aside — agent 走查报告

环境 https://aside.afsd.dev · 2026-07-30T19:26:08.166410Z · 走查 20 / 声明 77 条测试路径 · driver agent-playwright-mcp

这份报告在做什么：test-sweep 让 agent 读被测页面自己写的 .aup/man 知识，用 Playwright MCP 当真人逐条测试路径走查（导航/加标签/编辑/删除/创建…）， 每条路径各自一段录屏、每步截图并自判 verdict + 附观察证据（实际 DOM/文本/值，可复核）。 unreached=没驱动到（不当失败）；inconclusive=不确定（绝不判失败）。写操作默认真提交、不清理。
ℹ️ 覆盖 20 / 77：1 条声明路径未走到（可见但不阻断——漏走的补记为 unreached、不判红；绿灯只表示走到的都过了。agent 应据此自主决定再补哪些、完善报告）
  • tag-detail 动态入口 /app/tags/{tag}

未走到 navigate

precondition unmet: no existing 'active capture collection' in this account, and dd-11 session's attempt to self-create one by toggling 'Add to Capture Queue' during collection creation did not register (toggle click did not visibly flip state via any of role=switch/checkbox/selector/text targeting — see dd-11 step 11 fail note). Per feed man caveats and collections man doc, collection-capture-stop is confirm:true and explicitly gated behind a separate --commitDestructive flag ('so daily sweeps never touch real data') which this run does not have (only --commit-writes). Executing it against any pre-existing collection would risk mutating real account state outside the qa-tsweep self-created scope. Honestly recorded as unreached rather than faking a pass or touching non-self-created data.
